Gradient rank 34 of 42 rows => **multiplier polytope is 8-DIMENSIONAL** (all rows carry lambda >= 0.0476) => complementarity-form Jacobian **exactly singular 12/12**. Active-set sub-branch (|A|=34 vertex, min lambda 0.017542 = A22's 0.01754) gives n=69, cond 69, **isolates at h <= 2.4e-4** (half A22's r_N) in 0.20 s. (iv) **Honest (c2)** with lambda over the whole simplex [0,1]^(m+1) - what T3(c) literally asks - **0/30 excluded, 0/30 isolated, 20/30 singular**: interval Krawczyk on FJ is inoperative without multiplier information; sound repairs are an m-dim lambda B&B per selection or active-set enumeration (C(42,34)=1.2e8 for P1, C(49,34)=1.4e12 for P3, PER SELECTION). (v) **P2**: at s0 = shat+1e-6 all 14 offset cells die by (a) (matches A22's 155.7 bound); at s0 = shat+0.2 expansion eta >= 0.01 dies by the repaired (b), eta = 0.0026/0.005 leave exactly 1/8 of the menu alive. (vi) **P3** (loosest 5x45 family at 3.9, min gap 0.0039): h=0.01 -> **menu 4.03e8**, 60000 selections tested, 0 killed; h=0.001 -> menu 32768, 44% alive; h<=3e-4 -> (b). **D2 reach h_D2 ~ 0.015 (s0-s*)**, so **at s0 = s* D2 discharges nothing**. (vii) **Extrapolation.** Measured discharge radius `delta_kill/h` is SCALE-INVARIANT over a 50x range of h and two s0: min 1.15 / median **1.86** / max 3.75 => surviving cells per critical point ~ **c^33 = 7.8e8 (median), independent of h** - refinement shrinks the surviving volume but never the surviving cell COUNT. T3 can never close alone (a minimiser exists at s0 >= s*); composed with A22's box it must refine to h <= 1.75e-4, leaving a ~7.8e8-cell shell around Trump alone: **1.1e5 CPU-hours on the optimistic cheap path (which provably fails there), 4.9e7 CPU-hours realistically** - a floor, excluding the rest of D_0 and the other 35 A04 optima. Uniform cover for comparison: 1e63 cells at h=0.01. **The blow-up is dimensional, not algorithmic**: the per-branch engine costs 0.2 s. Recommended: (1) menu FACTORISATION (Cons(z) is a product over pairs, so per-pair covering sets K_p suffice: 131072 -> <=256); (2) branch on CONTACT STRUCTURE with the coordinate box held large, one square FJ system per structure solved globally (homotopy/Groebner) - removes c^33 entirely; (3) keep A24/A27 structure reduction on top, T3 only at the last level; (4) re-emit in t = tan(theta/2) (A28's blocker) to make the FJ systems algebraic. 16 gaps listed in results/pilot_report.json `findings.gaps` (G8: naive interval extension over-reports 'straddles activity', so every menu size quoted is an over-estimate; G11: D4 x S11 reduction not implemented; G12: no root-level B&B dive, so the non-critical part of D_0 is unmeasured). | attempts/A30-t3-pilot |

Flagged branches at k=7: lambda0=0 401250 (48% of MAIN), L3 self-stress 871. **NEW certified lemma (free): at most 3 squares per wall, not L5(c)'s 5** - two same-wall centres are within sqrt2/2 of it and >=1 apart, so their along-wall gap is >=0.97832 while the range is s-1<=2.8771; Trump's optimum has exactly 3/3/3/2, so it is TIGHT. **Maxwell |E|>=3k+1 is met by ZERO survivors at every k** (planarity caps |E| at 3k+2, observed max 3k-1) => flush contacts are mandatory for rigidity and |E|>=3k+1 must never be used as a prune. Level 1: gauge-fixed menu is 20 per SS edge / 2 per SW edge (the brief's 48/8 is a redundant un-gauge-fixed encoding); tilt propagation is EXACT for the tilt subsystem (flush=union-find, wall-flush=zero class, corner-on-edge=strict order; feasible iff the class digraph is acyclic and nothing sits below phi=0) and kills 79/88/94/96/98% at k=3..7, still leaving 4.1e10 decorations per graph at k=7. Level 1b: the ROTATION-SYSTEM (planar embedding) test the tilt pass ignores removes a further 2/5/21/50% (weighted) and 0/0/51/91% (median) at k=4..7 - the cheapest unexploited constraint, and it strengthens fast with k. Projection to k=11: 6.4e10-5.0e11 graphs x ~6.5e18 decorations = 4e29-3e30 structures; BEST case with L2 + per-wall-3 + embedding all promoted, ~5e9 x 5e15 = ~2.5e25. Even the undecorated Level-0 layer at 1 ms/graph is ~1e4 CPU-days. RECOMMENDATION: never enumerate a level - branch on structure jointly with the metric solve, promote L2 and the per-wall cap 3, build the rotation system into the generator, and treat the contact TYPE as an algebraic unknown inside ONE FJ solve per graph instead of 1e18 branches. Validation: rep counts = OEIS A000088; FULL independent brute force at k=2,3 (every edge subset) and k=4 (from L1 on) reproduces all 10 ablation rows + all 5 branch counts exactly; Burnside R0/R2 agrees; Level-1 exact counts match an independent brute-force tilt-ladder search on 24 cases; Level-1b MC matches the exact tilt rate; and **Trump's own contact graph passes every hard filter** (code/check_trump.py). k=8 measured cost ~120 core-h (not run). 14 gaps G1-G14 in results/count_report.json (biggest: Level 1 ignores ALL positional constraints, so the fan-out is an upper bound; rattler completion not counted; L7 is a statement about G not H). | attempts/A31-structure-count |

| A57-shadow-trees | computation postdoc (Opus) | kill k5_11 + k5_16 via A54's priced rung-3 sub-stratification (interior-first on the residual, depth <= 2, all banked levers incl. checkpoint-folding, exact certs, detached long runs) — the ~2-desktop-day item | A53-A55 machinery + A55/k3_ext E3 merged-warm column folding, lifted scale cap, column-only pruning, detached Start-Process, per-level progress files; NEW in A57: t-uniform theta cover, `pinguard` soundness gate, `alivewitness` disjoint-family arbiter, auto-scaling shard launcher | running (resumable + auto-scaling; L1 interior tree in flight) | **BOTH interior NO-children KILLED** — tau = 3.000000 vs budget 6 (eta = -3.000), exact certs total 21/5 < 6, re-verified independently at depth 60 => **Lemma A57-1: every 11-packing at s0 whose skeleton contains the k5_11 (resp. k5_16) pin set has a square centre in the interior zone [1.1, 2.777]^2**. **Two corrections to A54's rung-3 pricing.** (i) the theta cover must be uniform in t = tan(theta/2), not in degrees — the core half-side a = (1/2 - hd)/(1 + (t1-t0)) is set by the t-width, so a 12x12x8 cover gives a UNIFORM a = 0.35660 in 1152 children, where A54's degree-uniform cover gave a = 0.022-0.043 and priced the interior at ~6000; (ii) **SOUNDNESS GAP in A54 substrat.py, found and fixed** — a YES box whose core meets a pin core is EMPTY BY LOGIC (free kills: 344/1152 for k5_11, 257/1152 for k5_16) and a box CONTAINING a pin placement is UNSOUND at budget 11-k-1 (the pin itself may witness it); A54's single probe missed both cases by luck. Re-measured at a pinguard-OK box, an interior YES child converges to tau = 3.000000, eta = -2.0, 3 atoms, in 1-15 s (scale ceiling 1.667) — the YES side is cheap, not alive; an earlier a-curve reading of tau = budget exactly turned out to be a contradictory box and is RETRACTED (kept as T6b, the trap). **New arbiter banked** (`alivewitness.py`, closes the ALIVE half of GAP-A54-2 / GAP-A55-2): m pairwise-disjoint admissible unit squares with STRICTLY positive exact gaps, disjoint from every core, prove tau* >= budget => no certificate can ever exist. **Canary repaired:** A53 V6's tau*(trump5) >= 6 rested on an exact-touch family, which is one epsilon short for atomic measures; scaling Trump's free six to s0 by lambda = s0/s_T gives a strict exact gap 1.0574e-7 => trump5 LP-unkillable rigorously. trump5 never solved, never certified. **Depth 2 is the right and sufficient repair for the resisters:** the one level-1 child that converged AT budget (k5_16_interior_Y0017, tau = 5.000000000000014, minmass = maxcov = 1, 8.7 s) is a coarse box, not an obstruction - three bisections (a: 0.3566 -> 0.4233-0.4249) kill it 8/8 (four at tau = 4.000000 eta = -1.000, three at 3.000000 eta = -2.000, one at 3.500000 eta = -1.500; 322 s of LP total), and every sub-child keeps the SAME budget because splitting a YES box refines the same disjunction rather than opening a new branch (`code/deepen.py`). An alternative repair was tried and REJECTED with a measurement (`code/epsladder.py`, now opt-in and OFF): caselp keeps rows within EPS_CORE = 2e-3 of a core while `verify_case.py` exempts only what is PROVABLY exempt (exact, no epsilon), and 3 of the 5 weight-1 dual rows at that child sit in that shell - shrinking to 5e-4 does drop tau to 4.800000 but the certificate then FAILs on 19 exact holes (worst mass 0.4155) and the witness re-solve returns tau to 5.000000, so the shell rows are genuinely required. Live scoreboard: 25 children killed (10 exact certificates at level 1 + 7 logic kills + 8/8 at depth 2), 0 unresolved (the 3 remaining level-1 non-PASS records are queued for depth 2); running detached with a process-cap-aware auto-scaler (`code/scaleup.ps1`, <= 3 while A56's final_sweep lives, 5 after, then depth 2). **Third failure mode found and cured (T14):** a child with tau = 4.500000 and eta = -0.500 (ample margin) still FAILed exact verification with **0 HOLES** - 32, then 8, witness boxes the verifier could not DECIDE, sampled mass 1.2465 >= 1, atoms sitting on placement boundaries; more depth does NOT fix it (identical 8 undecided boxes at depth 56, 64 and 72, max depth pegged each time). A56's row-shrink margin cures it outright: `certify.py --eps_geo 0.002` re-solves at tau = 4.571429 (tau RISES - shrunk rows are strictly harder) and certifies total 4985907/1000000 < 5, PASS at round 0, independently re-verified at depth 60 with max depth used dropping from pegged-at-72-undecided to **32**. Now automatic in tree57.py before depth 2, giving three cost-ordered repairs: rc=3 tau=budget -> depth-2 refinement (8 sub-solves); rc=1 with 0 holes -> --eps_geo (~2 min); rc=1 with real holes -> more witness rounds then depth 2. **T19 (A60 relay, cross-campaign): COMMIT is the machine's binding constraint, not RAM.** Measured: a bare `python -c "import numpy"` reserves **628 MB of commit against a 27 MB working set**, so per-process baseline dominates the ~4x commit/WS overreserve A60 observed. Two causes, both fixed: (i) `verify_case.py` runs `mp.Pool(--jobs)`, making a certify at --jobs 2 into FOUR processes (~2.5 GB of commit in baselines alone) - `VERIFY_JOBS_CAP=1` takes verify_case's in-process branch and spawns nothing, saving ~1.3 GB per concurrent certify; (ii) Windows keeps the commit charge on freed heap blocks, so steady commit tracks PEAK numpy allocation - `A57_CHUNK` (default 2e6) cuts the two lpbox oracle temporaries from 80/160 MB to 16/32 MB. The chunk change is bit-for-bit result-neutral (each output element is a dot product over the full inner dimension, disjoint output slices) and was VERIFIED so: the k5_16 probe reproduced tau=3.000000, minmass/maxcov 1.000000, rows 144, orbits 3486 identically. Also adopted A60's defer(): tree57/deepen catch MemoryError, record to results/deferred_<key>.jsonl, sleep 30 s and continue WITHOUT marking the node done, so a transient spike can no longer destroy a shard's queue (T18 lost a shard and a deepen job together). scaleup.ps1 now gates on commit-available (Win32_OperatingSystem.FreeVirtualMemory) at 10.5 GB = ~4.5 GB per worker + a 6 GB cushion reserved for other campaigns. **BUG-LEDGER (A78 relay, T28): one FALSE artefact quarantined.** `results/alive/k5_16_m5.json` asserted "tau*(k5_16) >= 5 = budget => LP-unkillable" with budget 6 and m 5 - unsound as stated, since the disjoint-family bound needs m >= budget. Provenance is an A57 bug of a specific kind: the probe ran BEFORE alivewitness.py gained its `unkillable = bool(m >= budget)` guard, and the tool was fixed the same session while the already-written artefact was left on disk - **a guard added to an emitter does not retroactively correct output it has already produced**. Independent re-audit of all 11 files in results/alive/: exactly one is unsound; 9 are NO-WITNESS (assert nothing) and trump5_strict.json (m = 6 >= budget 6) is the only sound ALIVE-CERTIFIED witness. Moved to results/alive/quarantine/ with a README naming the defect (kept, not deleted). Nothing depends on it - G1 kills by exact certificate, no A57 code path reads results/alive/, and no campaign record cites an aliveness claim; the T7 prose already described it correctly as a lower bound, so the error never propagated beyond the artefact's own claim string. A78 confirms no soundness bug in the tool (alive_sound proved in Lean, core-monotone form). | attempts/A57-shadow-trees |

Proof it fired: with the shrink held fixed a feedback round only ADDS rows, so tau is nondecreasing - yet all SEVEN 'certify PASS but 4D open' rows PASSed at round >= 1 with tau FALLING (k6_21 4.600->4.500, k6_20 4.667->4.500, k5_10 5.588->5.500, k6_29 4.718->4.500, k5_17 5.400->5.333, k6_27 4.671->4.622, k5_05 flat at 5.000). 7 of 7. The fix is one flag pair A54 already carries for exactly this (`--eps_geo <eps> --no_resolve`). Soundness is untouched in either direction (the arbiter is the unmodified exact verifier, which never sees EPS_GEO), so no A56 PASS is invalidated - the leak cost COMPLETENESS. P6(v)'s '>= 0.0015 converts 13/16' now reads as 'a round-0 PASS keeps the full shrink'; the 12 survivors are exactly the rows that needed >= 1 feedback round and so never got the shrink they were priced for. (ii) **ATOM ENRICHMENT MOVES EVERY ROW (L2).** A56 read columns from ONE file (25-90 atoms) and then generated 4.9k-7.8k orbits that it checkpointed to `results/nodes/<name>_state.json` and never re-used; A61 folds every orbit the lab ever produced (warm57 extended to A56/A61 checkpoints) as the INITIAL pool. Measured tau0 (baseline shrink): k5_05 5.000 (slack 1.000, was 0.666), k5_17 5.333 (0.667, was 0.335), k5_10 5.500 / k6_20 4.500 / k6_21 4.500 / k6_29 4.500 (all exactly 0.500, were 0.077-0.365), k5_02 5.605 (0.395, was **0.00055** - a 700x lift), k5_12 5.620 (0.380), k6_27 4.621 (0.379), k5_03 5.667 (0.333). (iii) **L2b - THE ENRICHED GUARD LP SNAPS TO EXACT RATIONALS, AND THE MODAL VALUE IS THE GATE ITSELF.** Four of the six gate-clearing rows land on tau0 = budget - 1/2 to 12 significant figures; the rest on budget - 2/3 and budget - 1; the below-gate rows land on 281/50, 134/29, 17/3. Operational cost of learning this: A61's first pass compared `slack0 < 0.5` in floating point and threw away k5_10 (0.4999999999984732) and k6_21 (0.49999999999959144) - rows sitting exactly ON the law, refused on solver dust, ~25 min of LP each. The gate now carries a tolerance; any future P6-style threshold test against an LP optimum must too, because the interesting rows PILE UP at the threshold rather than scattering. (iv) **L5 - dtau/d(eps_geo) IS HALVED BY ENRICHMENT, SO P6(v)'s 0.5 GATE IS ~2x CONSERVATIVE.** Measured on the three conversions: 57.5 / 65.3 / 89.8 vs A56's 1.1-1.7e2, i.e. eps_geo = 0.003 costs 0.17-0.26 of budget, not 0.42. Mechanism: a shrunk row is only expensive when the measure has no ALTERNATIVE atom covering the shrunk square, and a rich column pool supplies them - the lever that drops tau0 also flattens the shrink curve. A61 therefore SPLITS the gate: a recalibrated pre-gate (0.30) on the extrapolated slack0, plus a POST-SHRINK gate on the MEASURED `budget - tau(eps_geo)` (0.03, the real condition for `total = tau*scale < budget` at scale >= 1.005). Abort-early is preserved exactly - no certify round is burnt on a row that provably cannot certify - but the test is now a measurement, and being wrong costs one stage-B LP (~15 min) instead of a ~1500 s certify burn. The four rows A56 and A61-pass-1 both refused (k5_02, k5_03, k5_12, k6_27, slack0 0.333-0.395) are re-running under it. (v) CONVERSIONS, all 4D depth-48 PASS over [191/50, 969271/250000] on the `gap` anchoring with C2 triple-PASS by the unmodified A53 verify_case.py at s_lo/mid/s0: k5_17 (11 atoms, total 1166001/200000, margin 0.170, 78.5 s), k6_20 (163 atoms, 2485329/500000, 0.029, 121.3 s), k6_29 (239 atoms, 623199/125000, 0.014, 103.7 s). Folded into the A56 scoreboard under the P7 ledger rule with full provenance (a61 block per row: warm sizes, tau0/slack0, tau at eps_geo, dtau/deps, certify, anchoring ladder, C2; original A53/A54 cert kept as the s0 witness, A61 parametric cert carries the window claim; backup `scoreboard.json.bak_pre_a61`). (vi) OPERATIONAL: A57 T18 RE-CONFIRMED - two `MemoryError: bad allocation` losses at ~10 GB free were BLAS oversubscription, not RAM; every worker now pins OPENBLAS/OMP/MKL/NUMEXPR to 1 thread before numpy/highspy import, plus `prune_cols=3` (A14 semantics - only zero-weight positive-reduced-cost columns, regenerable, cannot lower tau, so a pure memory bound) and a pool-stepping `lp_robust` ladder. THE CHAIN KILL NEVER FIRED at tree depth although its controls are clean (fires at r = 0.005/0.002, refuses r >= 0.0097 -- reproducing A111's r < s* - s0 = 0.0097 from the other side -- and refuses Trump at every radius): the trees reached only depth 3 (r = 0.0125) inside their wall, so depth 5-6 at r <= 0.006 is the one promising extension and it is a wall question. **k0 (6 un-killable A113 cells, four orderings): 0 kills, 0 certificates, 0 logic kills in 17-28 nodes each, because ALL 60 k0 LPs land at eta = 0 EXACTLY (none <= -1e-3)** -- removing a support placement from a k0 cell does not move tau, so the certifier is never invoked, and branching on corners does not by itself supply A113's missing logic kills. **The brief's k0 YES-side shortcut is UNSOUND as a box containment**: A53 stratifies by GRID-LOCKED (doubly flush, wall-anchored) squares, so a YES box inside a slot cell does not imply k >= 1; recorded (`class_clause`, `taken: false`) and never taken -- 37 opportunities, only 6 with a near-axis tilt. **THE PI's TIMEOUT PROTOCOL IS WORTH 2.26x** on the S02 LP wall (12,609 s vs 28,518 s under a flat 900 s cap on the same nodes; 55 s vs 124 s per LP over 230 LPs) and 1.06x on k0 where nothing is ever below budget; certification (631-1,807 s per ordering) is cheaper than the LP time it replaces. PIN WASTAGE vs ROW MASS: Spearman per branching node, median **+0.468** (S02, n = 45) / **+0.425** (k0, n = 34), positive in 84 %/82 % of nodes -- a genuinely different ranking, not a proxy, and neither better nor worse as an order at this sample size. BANKED: `certify.py --loop_cols` re-solves the node LP under a time limit before emitting, so **the certificate's atom set is wall-clock dependent** (A111's PASSing NO1 has 76 atoms; a 900 s re-emission produced 100 and did not verify) -- the frozen verifier is deterministic, the emission pipeline is not, so a kill is reproduced by REPLAYING its certificate, never by re-emitting it. Layer 1 = A213's Phase-2 FLOAT FILTER ported operation for operation to scalar C with the same rigorous forward error bound (one rounding per operation, hence -ffp-contract=off), three-valued: a pair it cannot decide falls through. Layer 2 = the EXACT test in integers -- __int128 for U, V, A, B, C and the interior test, SIGNED THREE-LIMB 192-BIT (i192.h) for the endpoint value (< 2^142) and the discriminant (< 2^183). Every magnitude is CHECKED against a proved envelope (|X|,|Y|,W,|CX|,|CY|,CW < 2^60, E,|T| < 2^31, |A|,|B|,|C| < 2^93); outside it the kernel returns UNDECIDED and Python's unbounded ints answer -- three fallback gates (no DLL / envelope / a t or corner that is not an exact double), so the kernel never decides what it cannot prove. `a214_cert_batch` does nv x nb pairs in one call (the entry point that matters); `tests/diff214.py`, `tests/all214.py`, `code/profrun214.py`, `code/stages214.py`, `results/timing214.md` | DONE (flag ON since the tests passed; the workers pick it up per node) | **[M] BIT-EXACT: 0 mismatches on 46,800 (vertex, box) samples layer by layer (the 192-bit path run on EVERY in-envelope sample, the filter separately, the full kernel, the batch, and 200 deliberately out-of-envelope points the kernel must refuse), on A213's replay of all 441 recorded rebuilds run twice (kernel OFF and ON) and on A213's fuzz (150 piece sets, 2,700 vertex tests, 75 polygon cases); A214_VERIFY=1 clean. PER CALL: certify_verts 2.0 -> 0.2 ms (x10 over A213's Python-int path, x321 over the library); the kernel alone is 16 ns a quadratic test / 263 ns a (vertex, box) pair against 655 ns / 10.5 us in Python -- exactly A213 s.6's prediction. The SCALAR drop-in is only x2.3 because a ctypes call costs ~1 us: the win is the batch. PER RUN (Y0202 pad 0, 900 s): the whole certification stage 3.5 -> 2.9 s while doing 19% MORE rebuilds (8.9 -> 6.2 ms a rebuild), 11 nodes expanded vs 9, the two frontier nodes the BEFORE run left ALIVE came back DEAD, verdicts identical on every node both runs finished; in that run the float filter decided 160,580 of 160,580 pairs in C (0 exact tests needed) and the fallback fired exactly where predicted (the axis core's corners, W ~ 2^110).
